 408+ 6A92                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_layout.asm"
# file opened: p127_layout.asm
   1++6A92              ; ============================================================================
   2++6A92              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6A92              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6A92              ;
   5++6A92              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6A92              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6A92              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6A92              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6A92              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6A92              ; ============================================================================
  11++6A92              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6A92              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6A92              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6A92              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6A92              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6A92              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6A92
  18++6A92              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6A92                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6A92 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6A95 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6A98 B7                   or      a
  23++6A99 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6A9B C2 DA 6A             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6A9E
  26++6A9E                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6A9E 21 E8 7A             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6AA1 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6AA4 B7                   or      a
  30++6AA5 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6AA7 DA DA 6A             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6AAA CA DA 6A             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6AAD 22 F4 6A             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6AB0 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6AB1 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6AB3 D2 DA 6A             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6AB6
  38++6AB6 21 E8 7A             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6AB9 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6ABC B7                   or      a
  41++6ABD ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6ABF DA C8 6A             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6AC2 CA C8 6A             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6AC5 C3 DA 6A             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6AC8              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6AC8                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6AC8 3A D7 71             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6ACB FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6ACD C2 DA 6A             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6AD0 3A D4 71             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6AD3 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6AD5 C2 DA 6A             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6AD8 B7                   or      a
  54++6AD9 C9                   ret
  55++6ADA
  56++6ADA              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6ADA 37                   scf
  58++6ADB C9                   ret
  59++6ADC
  60++6ADC              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6ADC              p127_layout_report:
  62++6ADC 21 F6 6A             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size
  63++6ADF CD 1F 62             call    p127_puts
  64++6AE2 3A F5 6A             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)
  65++6AE5 CD 3B 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  66++6AE8 3A F4 6A             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size)
  67++6AEB CD 3B 62             call    p127_put_hex8
  68++6AEE 21 12 6B             ld      hl,p127_msg_kernel_size_tail
  69++6AF1 C3 1F 62             jp      p127_puts
  70++6AF4
  71++6AF4              p127_kernel_size:
  72++6AF4 00 00                dw      0
  73++6AF6              p127_msg_kernel_size:
  74++6AF6 5B 50 31 32          db      "[P127] KERNEL IMAGE SIZE=0x",0
  74++6AFA 37 5D 20 4B
  74++6AFE 45 52 4E 45
  74++6B02 4C 20 49 4D
  74++6B06 41 47 45 20
  74++6B0A 53 49 5A 45
  74++6B0E 3D 30 78 00
  75++6B12              p127_msg_kernel_size_tail:
  76++6B12 20 42 59 54          db      " BYTES; P5/P2 BOUNDS PASS",13,10,0
  76++6B16 45 53 3B 20
  76++6B1A 50 35 2F 50
  76++6B1E 32 20 42 4F
  76++6B22 55 4E 44 53
  76++6B26 20 50 41 53
  76++6B2A 53 0D 0A 00
  77++6B2E
  78++6B2E              ASSERT P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES = 04000h
  79++6B2E              ASSERT p127_entry = P127_LAYOUT_START
  80++6B2E              ASSERT p127_end <= P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  81++6B2E

 410+ 6B2E              ; Keep the remap-critical code/data at the start of the Stage2 image so it
 411+ 6B2E              ; remains inside W1/P5. The preflight below verifies the actual linked bounds.
 412+ 6B2E                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6B2E              ; ============================================================================
   2++6B2E              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6B2E              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6B2E              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6B2E              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6B2E              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6B2E              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6B2E              ; ============================================================================
   9++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6B2E
  13++6B2E              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6B2E              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6B2E              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6B2E              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6B2E              p127_resident_start:
  18++6B2E
  19++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6B2E              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6B2E
  26++6B2E              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6B2E              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6B2E              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6B2E              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6B2E              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6B2E 32 B8 6B             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6B31 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6B32 32 B9 6B             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6B35 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6B36 32 BA 6B             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6B39 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6B3A 32 BB 6B             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6B3D
  39++6B3D 3A B8 6B             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6B40 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6B42 C2 6C 6B             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6B45 3A B9 6B             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6B48 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6B4A C2 6C 6B             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6B4D 3A BA 6B             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6B50 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6B52 C2 6C 6B             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6B55 3A BB 6B             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6B58 E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6B5A CA 6C 6B             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6B5D                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6B5D 3A B8 6B             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6B60 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6B62 CA 6C 6B             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6B65 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6B67 CA 6C 6B             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6B6A B7                   or      a
  58++6B6B C9                   ret
  59++6B6C
  60++6B6C              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6B6C 37                   scf
  62++6B6D C9                   ret
  63++6B6E
  64++6B6E              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6B6E              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6B6E              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6B6E 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6B70 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6B72 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6B74 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6B76 CD 2E 6B             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6B79 DA B6 6B             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6B7C
  74++6B7C 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6B7E 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6B80 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6B82 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6B84 CD 2E 6B             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6B87 D2 B6 6B             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6B8A
  81++6B8A 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6B8C 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6B8E 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6B90 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6B92 CD 2E 6B             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6B95 D2 B6 6B             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6B98
  88++6B98 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6B9A 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6B9C 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6B9E 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6BA0 CD 2E 6B             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6BA3 D2 B6 6B             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6BA6
  95++6BA6 3E 03                ld      a,3
  96++6BA8 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++6BAA 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++6BAC 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6BAE CD 2E 6B             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++6BB1 D2 B6 6B             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++6BB4 B7                   or      a
 102++6BB5 C9                   ret
 103++6BB6
 104++6BB6              p127_resident_test_fail:
 105++6BB6 37                   scf
 106++6BB7 C9                   ret
 107++6BB8
 108++6BB8              p127_resident_arg_target:
 109++6BB8 00                   db      0
 110++6BB9              p127_resident_arg_code:
 111++6BB9 00                   db      0
 112++6BBA              p127_resident_arg_stack:
 113++6BBA 00                   db      0
 114++6BBB              p127_resident_arg_flags:
 115++6BBB 00                   db      0
 116++6BBC
 117++6BBC
 118++6BBC              ; W3 ownership / UI quiesce state machine. Software only; no page OUT.
 119++6BBC              P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE EQU 1
 120++6BBC              P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED EQU 2
 121++6BBC              P127_W3_OWNER_UI EQU 1
 122++6BBC              P127_W3_OWNER_NONE EQU 0
 123++6BBC              P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF EQU 2
 124++6BBC
 125++6BBC              p127_w3_init:
 126++6BBC 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 127++6BBE 32 59 6D             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 128++6BC1 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 129++6BC3 32 5A 6D             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 130++6BC6 AF                   xor     a
 131++6BC7 32 5B 6D             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 132++6BCA B7                   or      a
 133++6BCB C9                   ret
 134++6BCC
 135++6BCC              p127_w3_ui_enter:
 136++6BCC 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 137++6BCF FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 138++6BD1 20 79                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 139++6BD3 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 140++6BD6 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 141++6BD8 20 72                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 142++6BDA 3A 5B 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 143++6BDD B7                   or      a
 144++6BDE 20 6C                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 145++6BE0 3E 01                ld      a,1
 146++6BE2 32 5B 6D             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 147++6BE5 B7                   or      a
 148++6BE6 C9                   ret
 149++6BE7
 150++6BE7              p127_w3_ui_leave:
 151++6BE7 AF                   xor     a
 152++6BE8 32 5B 6D             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 153++6BEB C9                   ret
 154++6BEC
 155++6BEC              p127_w3_quiesce:
 156++6BEC 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 157++6BEF FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 158++6BF1 20 59                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 159++6BF3 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 160++6BF6 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 161++6BF8 20 52                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 162++6BFA 3A 5B 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 163++6BFD B7                   or      a
 164++6BFE 20 4C                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 165++6C00 3E 02                ld      a,P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED
 166++6C02 32 59 6D             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 167++6C05 AF                   xor     a
 168++6C06 32 5A 6D             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 169++6C09 B7                   or      a
 170++6C0A C9                   ret
 171++6C0B
 172++6C0B              p127_w3_handoff_acquire:
 173++6C0B 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 174++6C0E FE 02                cp      P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED
 175++6C10 20 3A                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 176++6C12 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 177++6C15 B7                   or      a
 178++6C16 20 34                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 179++6C18 3E 02                ld      a,P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF
 180++6C1A 32 5A 6D             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 181++6C1D B7                   or      a
 182++6C1E C9                   ret
 183++6C1F
 184++6C1F              p127_w3_handoff_release:
 185++6C1F 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 186++6C22 FE 02                cp      P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED
 187++6C24 20 26                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 188++6C26 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 189++6C29 FE 02                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF
 190++6C2B 20 1F                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 191++6C2D AF                   xor     a
 192++6C2E 32 5A 6D             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 193++6C31 B7                   or      a
 194++6C32 C9                   ret
 195++6C33
 196++6C33              p127_w3_resume:
 197++6C33 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 198++6C36 FE 02                cp      P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED
 199++6C38 20 12                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 200++6C3A 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 201++6C3D B7                   or      a
 202++6C3E 20 0C                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 203++6C40 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 204++6C42 32 5A 6D             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 205++6C45 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 206++6C47 32 59 6D             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 207++6C4A B7                   or      a
 208++6C4B C9                   ret
 209++6C4C
 210++6C4C              p127_w3_reject:
 211++6C4C 37                   scf
 212++6C4D C9                   ret
 213++6C4E
 214++6C4E              p127_w3_selftest:
 215++6C4E CD BC 6B             call    p127_w3_init
 216++6C51 CD CC 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 217++6C54 DA 9B 6C             jp      c,p127_w3_test_fail
 218++6C57 CD EC 6B             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 219++6C5A D2 9B 6C             jp      nc,p127_w3_test_fail
 220++6C5D CD E7 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 221++6C60 CD EC 6B             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 222++6C63 DA 9B 6C             jp      c,p127_w3_test_fail
 223++6C66 CD CC 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 224++6C69 D2 9B 6C             jp      nc,p127_w3_test_fail
 225++6C6C CD 0B 6C             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 226++6C6F DA 9B 6C             jp      c,p127_w3_test_fail
 227++6C72 CD EC 6B             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 228++6C75 D2 9B 6C             jp      nc,p127_w3_test_fail
 229++6C78 CD CC 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 230++6C7B D2 9B 6C             jp      nc,p127_w3_test_fail
 231++6C7E CD 1F 6C             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 232++6C81 DA 9B 6C             jp      c,p127_w3_test_fail
 233++6C84 CD 33 6C             call    p127_w3_resume
 234++6C87 DA 9B 6C             jp      c,p127_w3_test_fail
 235++6C8A CD 33 6C             call    p127_w3_resume
 236++6C8D D2 9B 6C             jp      nc,p127_w3_test_fail
 237++6C90 CD CC 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 238++6C93 DA 9B 6C             jp      c,p127_w3_test_fail
 239++6C96 CD E7 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 240++6C99 B7                   or      a
 241++6C9A C9                   ret
 242++6C9B
 243++6C9B              p127_w3_test_fail:
 244++6C9B CD BC 6B             call    p127_w3_init
 245++6C9E 37                   scf
 246++6C9F C9                   ret
 247++6CA0
 248++6CA0              ; Save/restore the complete software W3 ownership tuple.
 249++6CA0              ; Snapshot and rollback are RAM-only; they never touch page registers.
 250++6CA0              p127_w3_snapshot:
 251++6CA0 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 252++6CA3 32 5C 6D             ld      (p127_w3_saved_state),a
 253++6CA6 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 254++6CA9 32 5D 6D             ld      (p127_w3_saved_owner),a
 255++6CAC 3A 5B 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 256++6CAF 32 5E 6D             ld      (p127_w3_saved_busy),a
 257++6CB2 B7                   or      a
 258++6CB3 C9                   ret
 259++6CB4
 260++6CB4              p127_w3_rollback:
 261++6CB4 3A 5C 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_saved_state)
 262++6CB7 32 59 6D             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 263++6CBA 3A 5D 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_saved_owner)
 264++6CBD 32 5A 6D             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 265++6CC0 3A 5E 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_saved_busy)
 266++6CC3 32 5B 6D             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 267++6CC6 B7                   or      a
 268++6CC7 C9                   ret
 269++6CC8
 270++6CC8              ; Recovery tests: reject a competing acquire, preserve a busy UI when
 271++6CC8              ; quiesce is refused, roll back a partial HANDOFF acquire, reject a
 272++6CC8              ; transition from HANDOFF, then resume and prove UI admission works again.
 273++6CC8              p127_w3_recovery_selftest:
 274++6CC8 CD BC 6B             call    p127_w3_init
 275++6CCB
 276++6CCB                      ; Acquisition from UI_ACTIVE/UI must fail without mutating state.
 277++6CCB CD A0 6C             call    p127_w3_snapshot
 278++6CCE CD 0B 6C             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 279++6CD1 D2 54 6D             jp      nc,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 280++6CD4 CD B4 6C             call    p127_w3_rollback
 281++6CD7 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 282++6CDA FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 283++6CDC C2 54 6D             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 284++6CDF 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 285++6CE2 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 286++6CE4 C2 54 6D             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 287++6CE7
 288++6CE7                      ; A busy UI cannot quiesce; rollback must retain the active lease.
 289++6CE7 CD CC 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 290++6CEA DA 54 6D             jp      c,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 291++6CED CD A0 6C             call    p127_w3_snapshot
 292++6CF0 CD EC 6B             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 293++6CF3 D2 54 6D             jp      nc,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 294++6CF6 CD B4 6C             call    p127_w3_rollback
 295++6CF9 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 296++6CFC FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 297++6CFE C2 54 6D             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 298++6D01 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 299++6D04 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 300++6D06 C2 54 6D             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 301++6D09 3A 5B 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 302++6D0C FE 01                cp      1
 303++6D0E C2 54 6D             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 304++6D11 CD E7 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 305++6D14
 306++6D14                      ; Quiesce, snapshot, acquire HANDOFF, reject an invalid transition,
 307++6D14                      ; then restore the complete pre-acquire state.
 308++6D14 CD EC 6B             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 309++6D17 DA 54 6D             jp      c,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 310++6D1A CD A0 6C             call    p127_w3_snapshot
 311++6D1D CD 0B 6C             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 312++6D20 DA 54 6D             jp      c,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 313++6D23 CD EC 6B             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 314++6D26 D2 54 6D             jp      nc,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 315++6D29 CD B4 6C             call    p127_w3_rollback
 316++6D2C 3A 59 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 317++6D2F FE 02                cp      P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED
 318++6D31 C2 54 6D             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 319++6D34 3A 5A 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 320++6D37 FE 00                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_NONE
 321++6D39 C2 54 6D             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 322++6D3C 3A 5B 6D             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 323++6D3F B7                   or      a
 324++6D40 C2 54 6D             jp      nz,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 325++6D43
 326++6D43                      ; Recovery must leave the UI usable, not merely restore bytes.
 327++6D43 CD 33 6C             call    p127_w3_resume
 328++6D46 DA 54 6D             jp      c,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 329++6D49 CD CC 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_enter
 330++6D4C DA 54 6D             jp      c,p127_w3_recovery_fail
 331++6D4F CD E7 6B             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 332++6D52 B7                   or      a
 333++6D53 C9                   ret
 334++6D54
 335++6D54              p127_w3_recovery_fail:
 336++6D54 CD BC 6B             call    p127_w3_init
 337++6D57 37                   scf
 338++6D58 C9                   ret
 339++6D59
 340++6D59 00           p127_w3_state: db 0
 341++6D5A 00           p127_w3_owner: db 0
 342++6D5B 00           p127_w3_busy: db 0
 343++6D5C 00           p127_w3_saved_state: db 0
 344++6D5D 00           p127_w3_saved_owner: db 0
 345++6D5E 00           p127_w3_saved_busy: db 0
 346++6D5F
 347++6D5F
 348++6D5F              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++6D5F              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++6D5F              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++6D5F              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++6D5F              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++6D5F              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++6D5F              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++6D5F              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++6D5F              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:
 357++6D5F F3                   di
 358++6D60                      ; Linked code and data must remain wholly inside the fixed W1 window.
 359++6D60 21 2E 6B             ld      hl,p127_resident_start
 360++6D63 11 00 60             ld      de,06000h
 361++6D66 B7                   or      a
 362++6D67 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 363++6D69 DA AC 6D             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 364++6D6C
 365++6D6C 21 AE 6D             ld      hl,p127_resident_end
 366++6D6F 11 00 80             ld      de,08000h
 367++6D72 B7                   or      a
 368++6D73 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++6D75 D2 AC 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 370++6D78
 371++6D78                      ; Representative mutable state must be within the resident module.
 372++6D78 21 59 6D             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 373++6D7B 11 2E 6B             ld      de,p127_resident_start
 374++6D7E B7                   or      a
 375++6D7F ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 376++6D81 DA AC 6D             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 377++6D84
 378++6D84 21 59 6D             ld      hl,p127_w3_state
 379++6D87 11 AE 6D             ld      de,p127_resident_end
 380++6D8A B7                   or      a
 381++6D8B ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++6D8D D2 AC 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++6D90
 384++6D90                      ; CALL has already pushed its return address. The live stack must
 385++6D90                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.
 386++6D90 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
 387++6D93 39                   add     hl,sp
 388++6D94 11 00 B0             ld      de,0B000h
 389++6D97 B7                   or      a
 390++6D98 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 391++6D9A DA AC 6D             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 392++6D9D
 393++6D9D 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
 394++6DA0 39                   add     hl,sp
 395++6DA1 11 00 C0             ld      de,0C000h
 396++6DA4 B7                   or      a
 397++6DA5 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 398++6DA7 D2 AC 6D             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 399++6DAA
 400++6DAA B7                   or      a
 401++6DAB C9                   ret
 402++6DAC
 403++6DAC              p127_resident_trampoline_fail:
 404++6DAC 37                   scf
 405++6DAD C9                   ret
 406++6DAE
 407++6DAE              p127_resident_end:

   2++6DAE              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++6DAE              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++6DAE              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++6DAE              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++6DAE              ; ============================================================================
   7++6DAE              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++6DAE              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++6DAE              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++6DAE              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++6DAE              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++6DAE              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++6DAE
  14++6DAE              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++6DAE              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++6DAE              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++6DAE              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++6DAE              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++6DAE              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++6DAE              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++6DAE              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++6DAE              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++6DAE              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++6DAE              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++6DAE
  26++6DAE              p127_irq_init:
  27++6DAE F3                   di
  28++6DAF 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++6DB1 32 90 70             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++6DB4 AF                   xor     a
  31++6DB5 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++6DB8 32 92 70             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++6DBB 32 93 70             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++6DBE B7                   or      a
  35++6DBF C9                   ret
  36++6DC0
  37++6DC0              ; OUT: carry clear only while the documented DI-only/no-vector contract holds.
  38++6DC0              p127_irq_validate:
  39++6DC0 3A 90 70             ld      a,(p127_irq_mask_state)
  40++6DC3 FE 01                cp      P127_IRQ_MASKED
  41++6DC5 C2 17 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  42++6DC8 3A 91 70             ld      a,(p127_irq_vector_owner)
  43++6DCB FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE
  44++6DCD C2 17 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  45++6DD0 3A 92 70             ld      a,(p127_irq_mode_policy)
  46++6DD3 FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET
  47++6DD5 C2 17 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  48++6DD8 B7                   or      a
  49++6DD9 C9                   ret
  50++6DDA
  51++6DDA              ; Fault-injection tests reject enabled-state, vector-owner and guessed-IM claims.
  52++6DDA              p127_irq_selftest:
  53++6DDA CD AE 6D             call    p127_irq_init
  54++6DDD CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
  55++6DE0 DA 19 6F             jp      c,p127_irq_test_fail
  56++6DE3 AF                   xor     a
  57++6DE4 32 90 70             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  58++6DE7 CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
  59++6DEA D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
  60++6DED 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  61++6DEF 32 90 70             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  62++6DF2 3E 01                ld      a,1
  63++6DF4 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  64++6DF7 CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
  65++6DFA D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
  66++6DFD AF                   xor     a
  67++6DFE 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  68++6E01 3E 01                ld      a,1
  69++6E03 32 92 70             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  70++6E06 CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
  71++6E09 D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
  72++6E0C CD AE 6D             call    p127_irq_init
  73++6E0F CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
  74++6E12 C9                   ret
  75++6E13
  76++6E13
  77++6E13              ; Readiness gate only. Carry is set unless vector ownership, mapped table,
  78++6E13              ; and resident handler are all explicitly committed. This routine NEVER EI.
  79++6E13              p127_irq_enable_preflight:
  80++6E13 3A 93 70             ld      a,(p127_irq_ready_flags)
  81++6E16 FE 07                cp      P127_IRQ_READY_ALL
  82++6E18 C2 17 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  83++6E1B 3A 91 70             ld      a,(p127_irq_vector_owner)
  84++6E1E B7                   or      a
  85++6E1F CA 17 6F             jp      z,p127_irq_reject
  86++6E22 3A 92 70             ld      a,(p127_irq_mode_policy)
  87++6E25 B7                   or      a
  88++6E26 CA 17 6F             jp      z,p127_irq_reject
  89++6E29 B7                   or      a
  90++6E2A C9                   ret
  91++6E2B
  92++6E2B              ; Prove the gate is closed by default and opens only for the complete
  93++6E2B              ; synthetic prerequisite tuple. Restore the real safe boot tuple afterward.
  94++6E2B              p127_irq_gate_selftest:
  95++6E2B CD AE 6D             call    p127_irq_init
  96++6E2E CD 13 6E             call    p127_irq_enable_preflight
  97++6E31 D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
  98++6E34 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR
  99++6E36 32 93 70             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
 100++6E39 CD 13 6E             call    p127_irq_enable_preflight
 101++6E3C D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
 102++6E3F 3E 07                ld      a,P127_IRQ_READY_ALL
 103++6E41 32 93 70             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
 104++6E44 3E 01                ld      a,1
 105++6E46 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
 106++6E49 3E 02                ld      a,2
 107++6E4B 32 92 70             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
 108++6E4E CD 13 6E             call    p127_irq_enable_preflight
 109++6E51 DA 19 6F             jp      c,p127_irq_test_fail
 110++6E54 CD AE 6D             call    p127_irq_init
 111++6E57 CD 13 6E             call    p127_irq_enable_preflight
 112++6E5A D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
 113++6E5D B7                   or      a
 114++6E5E C9                   ret
 115++6E5F
 116++6E5F              ; Stage 2.16 batch: exhaust the complete 3-bit readiness mask and all
 117++6E5F              ; prerequisite failure paths in one boot. This is a software contract test:
 118++6E5F              ; it does not model or claim an ATM interrupt source/vector/acknowledge cycle.
 119++6E5F              ; On success the real boot tuple is restored to DI + no owner + no mode.
 120++6E5F              p127_irq_batch_selftest:
 121++6E5F CD AE 6D             call    p127_irq_init
 122++6E62 CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
 123++6E65 DA 19 6F             jp      c,p127_irq_test_fail
 124++6E68
 125++6E68                      ; Exhaust all eight combinations of VECTOR/MAP/HANDLER readiness.
 126++6E68                      ; A nonzero synthetic owner/mode is supplied only for this gate test.
 127++6E68 3E 01                ld      a,1
 128++6E6A 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
 129++6E6D 32 92 70             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
 130++6E70 06 00                ld      b,0
 131++6E72              p127_irq_batch_mask_loop:
 132++6E72 78                   ld      a,b
 133++6E73 32 93 70             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
 134++6E76 CD 13 6E             call    p127_irq_enable_preflight
 135++6E79 D2 84 6E             jp      nc,p127_irq_batch_mask_open
 136++6E7C 78                   ld      a,b
 137++6E7D FE 07                cp      P127_IRQ_READY_ALL
 138++6E7F CA 19 6F             jp      z,p127_irq_test_fail
 139++6E82 18 06                jr      p127_irq_batch_mask_next
 140++6E84              p127_irq_batch_mask_open:
 141++6E84 78                   ld      a,b
 142++6E85 FE 07                cp      P127_IRQ_READY_ALL
 143++6E87 C2 19 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_test_fail
 144++6E8A              p127_irq_batch_mask_next:
 145++6E8A 04                   inc     b
 146++6E8B 78                   ld      a,b
 147++6E8C FE 08                cp      8
 148++6E8E 20 E2                jr      nz,p127_irq_batch_mask_loop
 149++6E90
 150++6E90                      ; All readiness bits without vector ownership must remain closed.
 151++6E90 3E 07                ld      a,P127_IRQ_READY_ALL
 152++6E92 32 93 70             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
 153++6E95 AF                   xor     a
 154++6E96 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
 155++6E99 3E 01                ld      a,1
 156++6E9B 32 92 70             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
 157++6E9E CD 13 6E             call    p127_irq_enable_preflight
 158++6EA1 D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
 159++6EA4
 160++6EA4                      ; All readiness bits without a selected policy must remain closed.
 161++6EA4 3E 01                ld      a,1
 162++6EA6 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
 163++6EA9 AF                   xor     a
 164++6EAA 32 92 70             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
 165++6EAD CD 13 6E             call    p127_irq_enable_preflight
 166++6EB0 D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
 167++6EB3
 168++6EB3                      ; Contract validator must reject each forbidden boot-state mutation.
 169++6EB3 CD AE 6D             call    p127_irq_init
 170++6EB6 AF                   xor     a
 171++6EB7 32 90 70             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
 172++6EBA CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
 173++6EBD D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
 174++6EC0
 175++6EC0 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
 176++6EC2 32 90 70             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
 177++6EC5 3E 01                ld      a,1
 178++6EC7 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
 179++6ECA CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
 180++6ECD D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
 181++6ED0
 182++6ED0 AF                   xor     a
 183++6ED1 32 91 70             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
 184++6ED4 3E 01                ld      a,1
 185++6ED6 32 92 70             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
 186++6ED9 CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
 187++6EDC D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
 188++6EDF
 189++6EDF                      ; Restore and verify the actual safe boot state; gate remains closed.
 190++6EDF CD AE 6D             call    p127_irq_init
 191++6EE2 CD C0 6D             call    p127_irq_validate
 192++6EE5 DA 19 6F             jp      c,p127_irq_test_fail
 193++6EE8 CD 13 6E             call    p127_irq_enable_preflight
 194++6EEB D2 19 6F             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail
 195++6EEE B7                   or      a
 196++6EEF C9                   ret
 197++6EF0
 198++6EF0              ; Validate the future IM2 table reservation and address geometry. The page
 199++6EF0              ; remains unmapped: W3 must still describe physical page 0 in the live map.
 200++6EF0              ; No table bytes are written, no I register is loaded, and no IM is selected.
 201++6EF0              p127_irq_table_preflight:
 202++6EF0 21 D6 71             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_IRQ_TABLE_PAGE
 203++6EF3 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 204++6EF4 FE 01                cp      1                       ; P127_MEM_RESERVED
 205++6EF6 C2 17 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
 206++6EF9 3A 24 79             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+P127_IRQ_TABLE_WINDOW)
 207++6EFC B7                   or      a
 208++6EFD C2 17 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject      ; W3 still maps bootstrap page 0
 209++6F00 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_IRQ_TABLE_BASE
 210++6F03 7C                   ld      a,h
 211++6F04 FE C0                cp      P127_IRQ_TABLE_I
 212++6F06 C2 17 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
 213++6F09 21 01 C1             ld      hl,P127_IRQ_TABLE_END
 214++6F0C 11 01 C1             ld      de,0C101h
 215++6F0F B7                   or      a
 216++6F10 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 217++6F12 C2 17 6F             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
 218++6F15 B7                   or      a
 219++6F16 C9                   ret
 220++6F17
 221++6F17                      ASSERT P127_IRQ_TABLE_WINDOW = 3
 222++6F17                      ASSERT P127_IRQ_TABLE_BASE = 0C000h
 223++6F17                      ASSERT P127_IRQ_TABLE_I = 0C0h
 224++6F17                      ASSERT P127_IRQ_TABLE_BYTES = 257
 225++6F17                      ASSERT P127_IRQ_TABLE_END = 0C101h
 226++6F17                      ASSERT P127_IRQ_TABLE_END <= 10000h
 227++6F17
 228++6F17              p127_irq_reject:
 229++6F17 37                   scf
 230++6F18 C9                   ret
 231++6F19              p127_irq_test_fail:
 232++6F19 CD AE 6D             call    p127_irq_init
 233++6F1C 37                   scf
 234++6F1D C9                   ret
 235++6F1E
 236++6F1E
 237++6F1E              ; Stage 2.15: resident interrupt frame protocol.
 238++6F1E              ; Hardware vector installation and IM selection are deliberately separate.
 239++6F1E              ; Entry contract: CPU has already pushed the interrupted PC; W1/P5 code and
 240++6F1E              ; W2/P2 stack remain mapped. The dispatcher may freely clobber all registers.
 241++6F1E              ; RETI restores IFF1 from IFF2; this routine never executes EI or IM.
 242++6F1E              p127_irq_entry:
 243++6F1E F5                   push    af
 244++6F1F C5                   push    bc
 245++6F20 D5                   push    de
 246++6F21 E5                   push    hl
 247++6F22 DD E5                push    ix
 248++6F24 FD E5                push    iy
 249++6F26 D9                   exx
 250++6F27 C5                   push    bc
 251++6F28 D5                   push    de
 252++6F29 E5                   push    hl
 253++6F2A D9                   exx
 254++6F2B 08                   ex      af,af'
 255++6F2C F5                   push    af
 256++6F2D 08                   ex      af,af'
 257++6F2E
 258++6F2E CD 43 6F             call    p127_irq_dispatch
 259++6F31
 260++6F31 08                   ex      af,af'
 261++6F32 F1                   pop     af
 262++6F33 08                   ex      af,af'
 263++6F34 D9                   exx
 264++6F35 E1                   pop     hl
 265++6F36 D1                   pop     de
 266++6F37 C1                   pop     bc
 267++6F38 D9                   exx
 268++6F39 FD E1                pop     iy
 269++6F3B DD E1                pop     ix
 270++6F3D E1                   pop     hl
 271++6F3E D1                   pop     de
 272++6F3F C1                   pop     bc
 273++6F40 F1                   pop     af
 274++6F41 ED 4D                reti
 275++6F43
 276++6F43              ; The source dispatcher is intentionally inert until an ATM interrupt
 277++6F43              ; source, acknowledgement policy and vector ownership are specified.
 278++6F43              ; The test-only branch aggressively clobbers both register banks so the
 279++6F43              ; frame round-trip can be checked without enabling asynchronous IRQs.
 280++6F43              p127_irq_dispatch:
 281++6F43 3A 7A 70             ld      a,(p127_irq_test_clobber)
 282++6F46 B7                   or      a
 283++6F47 C8                   ret     z
 284++6F48 3E EE                ld      a,0EEh
 285++6F4A 01 EE EE             ld      bc,0EEEEh
 286++6F4D 11 DD DD             ld      de,0DDDDh
 287++6F50 21 CC CC             ld      hl,0CCCCh
 288++6F53 DD 21 BB BB          ld      ix,0BBBBh
 289++6F57 FD 21 AA AA          ld      iy,0AAAAh
 290++6F5B D9                   exx
 291++6F5C 01 99 99             ld      bc,09999h
 292++6F5F 11 88 88             ld      de,08888h
 293++6F62 21 77 77             ld      hl,07777h
 294++6F65 D9                   exx
 295++6F66 08                   ex      af,af'
 296++6F67 3E DD                ld      a,0DDh
 297++6F69 B7                   or      a
 298++6F6A 08                   ex      af,af'
 299++6F6B C9                   ret
 300++6F6C
 301++6F6C              ; CALL-based synthetic entry test. It saves the caller's complete register
 302++6F6C              ; set, loads sentinels into primary/alternate banks, invokes the same entry
 303++6F6C              ; routine used by a future vector, and verifies exact register restoration.
 304++6F6C              ; DI is mandatory here: RETI copies IFF2 to IFF1, and both must remain zero.
 305++6F6C              p127_irq_context_selftest:
 306++6F6C F3                   di

 332++6F97 D9                   exx

   1++7094              ; ============================================================================
   2++7094              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7094              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++7094              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7094              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7094              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7094              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7094              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7094              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7094              ; ============================================================================
  11++7094              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++7094              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++7094              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3

  16++7094              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0

   1++7212              ; ============================================================================
   2++7212              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++7212              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7212              ; ABI:
   5++7212              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++7212              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++7212              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++7212              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++7212              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++7212              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++7212              ; ============================================================================
  12++7212              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  13++7212              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++7212              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2

  16++7212

  20++7215 11 24 73             ld      de,p127_range_length

  28++7221 1A                   ld      a,(de)

  30++7223 7E                   ld      a,(hl)

   1++732C              ; ============================================================================
   2++732C              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++732C              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++732C              ;
   5++732C              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++732C              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++732C              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++732C              ;
   9++732C              ; API:
  10++732C              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++732C              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++732C              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++732C              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++732C              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++732C              ;
  16++732C              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++732C              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++732C              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++732C              ; ============================================================================
  20++732C              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++732C              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++732C              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++732C              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++732C              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++732C

  27++732C              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++732C              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.

   1++748A              ; ============================================================================
   2++748A              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++748A              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++748A              ;
   5++748A              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++748A              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++748A              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++748A              ;
   9++748A              ; API:
  10++748A              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++748A              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++748A              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++748A              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++748A              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++748A              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++748A              ; ============================================================================
  17++748A              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++748A              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++748A              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0

  21++748A              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++748A              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh

 178++7857              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++7857              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++7857              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++7857              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++7857              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++7857              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++7857              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++7857              p127_map_handoff_prepare:

   1++7A4D              ; ============================================================================
   2++7A4D              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++7A4D              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7A4D              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++7A4D              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.
   6++7A4D              ; ============================================================================
   7++7A4D              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++7A4D              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
   9++7A4D              P127_HANDOFF_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++7A4D
  11++7A4D              ; Current bootstrap stack is at #BFFF in W2/P2. Keep at least 128 bytes
  12++7A4D              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.
  13++7A4D              P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP    EQU 0B900h
  14++7A4D              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h
  15++7A4D              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP     EQU 0B880h
  16++7A4D              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO      EQU 0B7FEh
  17++7A4D              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++7A4D
  19++7A4D              ; IN: none.
  20++7A4D              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++7A4D              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++7A4D              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++7A4D              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++7A4D              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++7A4D              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.
  26++7A4D              p127_handoff_stack_probe:
  27++7A4D F3                   di
  28++7A4E F5                   push    af
  29++7A4F C5                   push    bc
  30++7A50 D5                   push    de
  31++7A51 E5                   push    hl
  32++7A52 DD E5                push    ix
  33++7A54 FD E5                push    iy
  34++7A56 ED 73 E5 7A          ld      (p127_handoff_saved_sp),sp
  35++7A5A
  36++7A5A                      ; Refuse if the live stack has crossed its declared low-water limit.
  37++7A5A 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
  38++7A5D 39                   add     hl,sp
  39++7A5E 11 00 B9             ld      de,P127_HANDOFF_MIN_OLD_SP
  40++7A61 B7                   or      a
  41++7A62 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++7A64 DA B5 7A             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_refuse
  43++7A67
  44++7A67                      ; Canary bytes bracket the unused gap below the scratch stack.
  45++7A67 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
  46++7A69 32 FE B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO),a
  47++7A6C 3E 5A                ld      a,05Ah
  48++7A6E 32 FF B7             ld      (P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI),a
  49++7A71
  50++7A71                      ; Switch only the software stack pointer. W2/P2 stays mapped.
  51++7A71 31 80 B8             ld      sp,P127_HANDOFF_STACK_TOP
  52++7A74 21 57 13             ld      hl,01357h
  53++7A77 E5                   push    hl
  54++7A78 11 68 24             ld      de,02468h
  55++7A7B D5                   push    de
  56++7A7C CD CD 7A             call    p127_handoff_stack_inner
  57++7A7F DA AD 7A             jp      c,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  58++7A82 D1                   pop     de
  59++7A83 21 68 24             ld      hl,02468h
  60++7A86 B7                   or      a
  61++7A87 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  62++7A89 C2 AD 7A             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  63++7A8C E1                   pop     hl
  64++7A8D 11 57 13             ld      de,01357h
  65++7A90 B7                   or      a
  66++7A91 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  67++7A93 C2 AD 7A             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  68++7A96
  69++7A96 3A FE B7             ld      a,(P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_LO)
  70++7A99 FE A5                cp      0A5h
  71++7A9B C2 AD 7A             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  72++7A9E 3A FF B7             ld      a,(P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI)
  73++7AA1 FE 5A                cp      05Ah
  74++7AA3 C2 AD 7A             jp      nz,p127_handoff_stack_fail
  75++7AA6
  76++7AA6 AF                   xor     a
  77++7AA7 32 E7 7A             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  78++7AAA C3 BA 7A             jp      p127_handoff_stack_restore
  79++7AAD
  80++7AAD              p127_handoff_stack_fail:
  81++7AAD 3E 01                ld      a,1
  82++7AAF 32 E7 7A             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  83++7AB2 C3 BA 7A             jp      p127_handoff_stack_restore
  84++7AB5
  85++7AB5              p127_handoff_stack_refuse:
  86++7AB5 3E 02                ld      a,2
  87++7AB7 32 E7 7A             ld      (p127_handoff_result),a
  88++7ABA
  89++7ABA              p127_handoff_stack_restore:
  90++7ABA                      ; Restore the exact saved SP before touching the caller's saved frame.
  91++7ABA ED 7B E5 7A          ld      sp,(p127_handoff_saved_sp)
  92++7ABE FD E1                pop     iy
  93++7AC0 DD E1                pop     ix
  94++7AC2 E1                   pop     hl
  95++7AC3 D1                   pop     de
  96++7AC4 C1                   pop     bc
  97++7AC5 F1                   pop     af
  98++7AC6 3A E7 7A             ld      a,(p127_handoff_result)
  99++7AC9 B7                   or      a
 100++7ACA C8                   ret     z
 101++7ACB 37                   scf
 102++7ACC C9                   ret

 104++7ACD              ; Nested CALL/PUSH/POP test on the scratch stack; BC is preserved.
 105++7ACD              p127_handoff_stack_inner:
 106++7ACD C5                   push    bc
 107++7ACE 01 5A A5             ld      bc,0A55Ah
 108++7AD1 C5                   push    bc

 116++7ADF C1                   pop     bc

  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret

 239+ A149              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.

 248+ A153                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 249+ A153                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.

 251+ A154 D1                   pop     de

 513+ A311              ; Move log rows 13..24 up by one. Each hardware text row has a 64-byte
 514+ A311              ; stride; the 40 visible bytes are copied together with the row padding.
 515+ A311              P126_ScrollConsoleUp:

 523+ A318 21 00 45             ld      hl, #4500

   1+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A3F2              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A3F2              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A3F2              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A3F2              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A3F2              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A3F2              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A3F2              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A3F2
  15+ A3F2              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A3F2              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A3F2              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A3F2              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A3F2              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A3F2              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A3F2              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:

 398+ A70D              p127_physmem_cleanup_free:

 420+ A736              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:

 440+ A760 B7                   or      a
 441+ A761 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A763                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A763                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A763                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.

 452+ A76A              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A76A              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A76A              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A76A                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A76A                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A76A                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A76A                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.

 573+ A8A4 37                   scf

 575+ A8A6
 576+ A8A6              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A8A6              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ A8A6              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ A8A6 21 D2 71             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ A8A9 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ A8AB 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ A8AD              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ A8AD 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ A8AE FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A8B0 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A8B2 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A8B4 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A8B6 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ A8B8 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ A8BA FE 01                cp      1
 591+ A8BC 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ A8BE FE 02                cp      2
 593+ A8C0 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ A8C2 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ A8C4 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ A8C6 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ A8C8 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ A8CA FE 05                cp      5
 599+ A8CC 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ A8CE 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ A8D0              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ A8D0 C5                   push    bc
 603+ A8D1 E5                   push    hl
 604+ A8D2 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ A8D3 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ A8D5 21 D2 71             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ A8D8 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ A8D9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
